## youtube-live — YouTube Live

- [ ] Confirm GET /youtube/v3/ answers without spending quota and without checking the token (a 404), so idle probes are free. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:183](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L183))
- [ ] Confirm an expired or revoked access token answers 401 on every method, and that quota and permission failures answer 403. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:179](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L179))
- [ ] Confirm transition, bind and cuepoint answer 200 (the reference gives no status), and that a transition's reply is the broadcast. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:271](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L271))
- [ ] Confirm the deletes (broadcasts, streams, chat messages, bans) answer 204. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:361](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L361))
- [ ] Record the limits on an ad break's durationSecs and a temporary ban's banDurationSeconds (the reference gives none). ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:324](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L324))
- [ ] Record the longest live chat message YouTube accepts (the reference gives no limit). ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:447](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L447))
- [ ] Check that lastUpdateTimeSeconds and concurrentViewers arrive as JSON strings, as Google's 64-bit integers do. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:942](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L942))
- [ ] Confirm that a stream's health status and configuration issues are reported while it is active and bound, and how soon after a change. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:943](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L943))
- [ ] Confirm a refresh at https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token with client_id alone (an installed-app client, no client_secret) succeeds, and that Google never rotates the refresh token on refresh. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:174](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L174))
- [ ] Confirm a refresh token from a project in publishing status Testing fails with 400 invalid_grant after 7 days, and that a revoked one answers the same. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:694](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L694))
- [ ] Record the access token lifetime Google gives (expires_in, about 3599 s expected), so the 300 s refresh margin is right. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:175](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L175))
- [ ] Confirm pageInfo.totalResults on liveStreams.list with mine=true counts every stream of the channel: when it is 50 or fewer, the poll's page replaces streams. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:963](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L963))
- [ ] Confirm the quota costs this spec now gives (every liveBroadcasts and liveStreams method 1 unit; chat list 5, insert, delete and transition 20; bans 200; chat moderators list 50, insert and delete 200) against the units the Cloud console counts. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:707](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L707))
- [ ] Confirm videos.list with part snippet,status,liveStreamingDetails,statistics still costs 1 unit, and that a broadcast's video answers status.madeForKids and containsSyntheticMedia. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:893](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L893))
- [ ] Confirm videos.update with part status alone keeps the snippet, and that a status body leaving out publicStatsViewable or embeddable resets them, as the reference says. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:618](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L618))
- [ ] Confirm videos.update answers the video resource (kind youtube#video) with the parts asked for. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:596](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L596))
- [ ] Confirm the moderator id liveChatModerators.list returns is the id liveChatModerators.delete takes, and that its characters are letters, digits, - and _ only. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:535](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L535))
- [ ] Confirm liveChatModerators.list answers pageInfo.totalResults, so a list of 50 or fewer replaces chat_moderators. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:1020](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L1020))
- [ ] Confirm a pollEvent insert with 2 to 4 options answers the message with its id, and record the longest question and option YouTube accepts. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:545](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L545))
- [ ] Confirm close_chat_poll answers the message with status closed and each option's tally as a numeric string. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:563](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L563))
- [ ] Confirm superChatEvents.list answers newest first and amountMicros as a JSON string. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:574](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L574))
- [ ] Confirm thumbnails.set accepts the image as the whole request body with uploadType=media (the reference names the upload URL but not the upload type), answers 200 with the thumbnails set, and refuses a channel that may not use custom thumbnails with 403. ([specs/youtube-live.yaml:642](specs/youtube-live.yaml#L642))

## Integrations with no open items

Nothing specific is listed for these yet, but test results are still welcome: generic-http, generic-osc, generic-tcp-udp, generic-websocket, http-listener, http-snapshot, obs-studio.
